## `AGO-6020-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-02`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Signal Inspector
- **Scenario:** Raw Material Batch Quality Verification, NDT Inspection & Defect Failure Analysis SOP for Baseband Video SDI Eye Pattern & Black-Frame/Silence Defect

## `ALB-6020-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Role:** OB-Van-Vision-Mixer Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for 4K/8K UHD Mobile Broadcast OB Van Video Switcher Vision Mixer
